Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in fundamental_group theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 763 and the invariant I(A,B) = 972 mod 4.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the fundamental_group decomposition. By minimality, the element a^763 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 972-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
